FAQs
What is a teaching tool?
A teaching tool is a resource that educators use to enhance the learning experience of students. These tools aim to improve student engagement, understanding, and retention of learning material.
How can I make my teaching interactive?
To make your teaching interactive, you can implement various tools, such as:
Interactive educational quizzes
Gamified learning experiences
AI-enhanced tools
Collaborative learning solutions, like Kahootopia!
Interactive presentations
What is an example of interactive teaching?
An example of interactive teaching is using interactive quizzes and games, such as those created with Kahoot!
Teachers can design engaging quizzes to make learning fun and interactive, which helps consolidate knowledge while keeping students motivated and involved in the learning process.
What is Kahoot!?
Kahoot! is a game-based learning platform that makes it easy to create, share and play learning games or trivia quizzes in minutes.
How can Kahoot! be used in schools?
Kahoot! can be used in schools for:
Interactive learning: Teachers can create engaging quizzes to make learning fun and interactive, whilst simultaneously consolidating knowledge.
Advanced question types: Master learning content with access to our precision tools, including pin answers, slider questions, type answers, and puzzles.
Varied media integration: Teachers can incorporate various types of media into their Kahoot! quizzes and presentations, including images, videos, and audio files.
Detailed reports and analytics: Check your class’s learning and adapt your planning with downloadable reports, perfect for gathering insights into students’ understanding and identifying knowledge gaps.
AI-enhanced creator tools: Try our AI-assisted kahoot generator to instantly create lesson content from any topic, PDF, URL, or Wikipedia article.
Can Kahoot! be used for remote learning?
Yes, Kahoot! can be used for remote learning. The platform supports hosting kahoots remotely using video calling tools or live streaming services, allowing players to join from anywhere in the world.
How does Kahoot! improve student engagement?
Kahoot! improves student engagement through interactive and gamified learning experiences. Teachers can conduct real-time assessments, providing immediate feedback to students, helping identify areas of improvement, and keeping students engaged by addressing their learning needs promptly. What’s more, Kahoot! offers a variety of question types that allow teachers to cater to the different learning needs of students while keeping lessons stimulating and engaging.
Are there any free plans for schools?
Yes, we offer Kahoot! Go, a free plan for teachers. It provides the essential tools for creating engaging lessons, motivating students, and tracking progress. Click here to explore the full list of features available with Kahoot! Go.
Why is student engagement important?
Student engagement is important because it:
Increases motivation and participation.
Enhances retention and understanding of learning material.
Improves academic performance.
Provides valuable insights into student progress and informs planning.
Promotes a positive attitude towards learning.
Research has shown that using interactive tools like Kahoot! can improve students’ learning outcomes, reduce anxiety associated with assessments, and provide valuable insights for teachers to tailor their lessons to meet students’ needs.
Engaged students are also more likely to enjoy the learning process, which can lead to a more positive attitude towards education and a lower risk of falling behind or dropping out.
How to create quizzes using Kahoot!?
Here’s a quick guide on how to create quizzes using Kahoot!:
Log in to your Kahoot! account. If you don’t have an account, you’ll need to sign up for one.
Click the Create button. This will open the kahoot creator.
In the creator, you can use AI to create your kahoot. Quickly turn handwritten notes, PDFs, Wikipedia articles, or slides into a lesson.
Customize your questions and reorganize the order of your questions, should you wish to.
Click Done to save your kahoot. You can then preview it to see how it will look to players.
To host your kahoot live, click on the Host live button.
To assign it, click on Assign and set a deadline for completion.
Share your kahoot by giving your students the game PIN or a direct link. They can also scan a QR to join.
What is the best tool for formative assessment?
Kahoot! is an excellent formative assessment tool. It offers interactive question types, immediate feedback, detailed reports, and insights into student performance, which help teachers identify learning gaps and tailor their lessons accordingly. Additionally, its gamified nature keeps students engaged and motivated.

Book an intro of Kahoot! EDU
Kahoot! for schools
Interactive lessons
Create and teach interactive lessons in class, virtually or in a hybrid format, engage students and increase participation. No need to switch between multiple tools – you can teach an entire lesson with your kahoot!
Get Kahoot!+ Book an intro
Get started for free
Log in
How to use interactive lessons with Kahoot! in class and beyond
Introduce new topics
In the beginning of your kahoot, present some key points to introduce new content so students have a better idea of what to expect in this lesson.
Instruct in class and virtually
Kahoot! can be used to teach interactive lessons and engage students both in class and in virtual or hybrid learning.
Pre-assess knowledge
Gather insights that will help you plan your interactive lessons in the best possible way, aligned with where a class currently stands.
Increase participation
Increase focus and motivate students to participate with interactive questions such as quiz, poll, type answer, and more.
Instantly assess learning
With real-time reports, Kahoot! can help you instantly assess how the class feels about a topic so you can adjust your interactive lesson.
Recap on learning content
Add a slide with key points of topics you’ve covered to help students remember the most important information from the lesson.
Reinforce knowledge after lessons
Assign student-paced challenges that learners can complete to study and practice to reinforce knowledge after your lesson.
Foster students' creativity
Encourage students to create their own kahoots. It’s a great way to improve their creativity, research and presentation skills!
“Kahoot! works great for virtual lessons when school is closed.”
Ashley Kollar, 3rd grade teacher, Beekmantown Central School District
Read success story
“Teaching with Kahoot! keeps students engaged and shakes up the usual assignments.”
Steve Auslander, 5th grade teacher, Indianapolis
Read success story
Top Kahoot! features for creating and teaching interactive lessons
These features will support you before, during and after your lesson to maximize learning:
Add slides - free for all K-12 teachers and students!
Add slides to your kahoot to introduce new topics, provide additional context, or recap on the key points. With a free teacher or students account, you get access to classic slides. In this layout, you can add a short headline, an image and a short explanatory text under it. By upgrading your teacher account, you can unlock advanced slide layouts which will help you get even more creative with learning content.
Import existing slides to save time
Have a good presentation that is a perfect fit for your next lesson? By upgrading your account, you can easily import slides created in PowerPoint, Keynote, Google Slides, or from a PDF file straight into your kahoots and add interactive questions between slides.
Host kahoots directly from your PowerPoint presentation
Are you using PowerPoint in your lessons? With our new integration, you can now start a live kahoot directly from your presentation. Whether you teach in class, virtually or in a hybrid format, with our new add-in, you can make your lesson truly interactive by bringing your favorite tools together!
Deepen learning and increase participation with interactive questions
Depending on your subscription, you can combine slides with various question types in one interactive lesson. In addition to multiple-choice quiz, you can add polls (perfect for quick pulse-checks), puzzles (test accuracy by asking students to arrange answers in the correct order), type answer (check knowledge without cues), word cloud and open-ended questions (give students a voice and collect in-depth feedback).
Create lesson plans with kahoot collections
Add several kahoots to a collection that serves as a playlist. For example, you can organize a collection around a specific topic or chapter in the textbook. You can supplement it with a PDF and use it as a plan for a future lesson.
Easily teach via video
Host a kahoot live in class or via a video conferencing tool to teach an interactive lesson. Questions and answers are displayed on a shared screen while students answer on their devices but you can choose to display questions on their devices – which works perfectly in a virtual learning environment.
Control lesson dynamics with additional options
There are numerous features allowing you to control lesson dynamics depending on the goals, for example, controlling the volume when explaining a complex topic. Enabling double points in questions will add extra motivation. Image reveal within questions will get students extra focused and build anticipation.
Send student-paced challenges
In a student-paced challenge, learners see questions and answers on their screen. You can turn off the question timer to promote accuracy, which makes this feature particularly helpful to review content and reinforce knowledge after an interactive lesson.
Watch video tutorial: how to create slides in Kahoot!

Frequently asked questions
Is the pricing listed per team or per teacher?
Unless specified otherwise in a special offer or promotion, price is listed per teacher.
Can someone else pay for my subscription?
You can request a Kahoot!+ Voucher. All you need to do is generate a link and share it with someone who is eager to help. This allows them to pay for Kahoot! subscription of your selection. You can generate the link in the Billing tab of your account settings.
Do you offer both monthly and annual billing?
The prices displayed on our website include both the annual cost and the monthly breakdown of the annual subscription. However, all these plans are billed upfront for the entire year. Currently, there is no option for monthly billing for any of our educational plans.
Is Word Cloud, Open-Ended Questions and Brainstorm included in my subscription?
These features are not currently available with our current individual K-12 school plans due to Children’s Data Privacy laws.
If you wish to use the aforementioned features you can upgrade to our EDU subscription.
What are the participant limits per game?
The participants limit differs based on what plan you’re on:
Go: Up to 10 participants per game
Teacher essentials: 40 participants per game
Bronze: Up to 50 participants per game
Silver: Up to 100 participants per game
Gold: Up to 200 participants per game
I’d like to collaborate with my colleagues. Is there a team plan available?
Absolutely! At a discounted price, admins can empower and connect educators across a school or district with versatile tools for blended and distance learning. Learn more about a Kahoot! EDU site license and request a personalized quote here.
I can't find my content, it was moved to another workspace. What is a workspace and how does it work?
With workspaces, you can organize your content and separate what’s shared with your team and what’s private. A workspace is where you can create, edit, and access kahoots, courses, reports, and groups. You can switch between workspaces anytime. Access to content and features differs based on which workspace you’re in. Learn more about Workspaces here.
What is AccessPass and how does it work?
Kahoot!+ AccessPass is the first premium content subscription, representing the launch of a global, digital marketplace on Kahoot!. The new subscription provides access to a comprehensive library of premium ready-to-use resources from Kahoot!, on a wide range of topics for all ages, to take learning, teaching, and having fun in any environment to the next level!
With Kahoot!+ AccessPass, you get:
Unlimited access to premium content for learning, entertainment, and engagement.
Full library with thousands of ready-made games and resources including exclusive collections.
Trusted content from verified creators and partners is added monthly.
Can I add students to a group?
Yes, you can add students to your groups! You can find more information about groups here.
Young students (U.S. students under 13 and non-U.S. students under 16) are unable to join or create groups.
What are student passes?
Student pass is a function that allows teachers to easily share access to our premium functionality geared towards students, including learning apps and enhanced creator functionality, by simply sharing a QR code or a link. More information here.
Where can I find Kahoot!’s W-9 form?
Download our signed W-8-BEN-E form here.
Does Kahoot! still offer a free version?
Yes, we offer Kahoot! Go, a free plan for teachers. It provides the essential tools for creating engaging lessons, motivating students, and tracking progress. Click here to explore the full list of features available with Kahoot! Go.
How do I get a verified Kahoot! Academy profile?
Apply to become a Verified educator and join the Kahoot! Academy community and marketplace for educators.
Does Kahoot! provide a sole source letter?
Please send us a request to schools@kahoot.com.
Can I use Kahoot! in languages other than English?
Yes! You can write questions and answers in any language to create a native language experience for participants. You can also play, create and host kahoots, view reports and more in Spanish, Brazilian Portuguese, French, German, Italian, Japanese, Dutch, Polish, Turkish, Norwegian, Swedish, Danish, Arabic, Ukrainian, Korean, Indonesian, Simplified Chinese, Traditional Chinese, Thai and Vietnamese. More languages are coming soon to our apps and website!
What is type answer question?
Kahoot! now supports a variety of open-ended question types to eliminate guesswork and test how well learners recall content without cues by asking them to type the correct short answer:
With type answer questions, you can test how well learners recall content without cues by asking them to type the correct short answer! Participants type in an answer which is validated instantly.
Can I get a site license?
Absolutely! At a discounted price, admins can empower and connect educators across a school or district with versatile tools for blended and distance learning. Learn more about a Kahoot! EDU site license and request a personalized quote here.
Are students able to share kahoots?
Yes, students above certain age can share their content without any constraints.
Young student accounts (U.S. students under 13 and non-U.S. students under 16) can present their own kahoots live to their class, but they cannot share (make public, share via link or with another account) their content.
If you want your children or students to share their question ideas with you, you can use the import questions from template feature.
My school is tax exempt. Why was I charged tax and how can I get rid of it?
We are currently updating our systems to reflect our customers’ tax-exemption status correctly. If you were charged tax while your organization is tax-exempt, please contact our Support team so that we can adjust this. We will need to confirm some details with you and will request the tax exemption certificate so it is recommended to have it prepared
How do I downgrade/upgrade my subscription?
To upgrade or downgrade, subscribe to a higher or lower tier plan on this pricing page or through prompts in the platform. Read more about upgrades and downgrades here.
You can upgrade anytime with an immediate effect.
I can't find my current plan in the offer.
Do you have an active school teacher subscription that isn’t visible on the pricing pages? Check the features of school (K-12) legacy plans here.

Kahoot! Resources
Welcome to the Kahoot! Library where you can find resources about professional development, our webinars and helpful guides to get you started.
Research and awards | For schools | For business
Research
The Kahoot! learning experience
Kahoot! is more than just a quiz tool, it’s an evidence-based platform educators can leverage to engage students, reinforce learning, and create interactive experiences that boost student grades.
Learn more
Awards and recognition
Kahoot! has been proudly backed by independent research and renowned certifications, demonstrating that using Kahoot! significantly enhances students’ learning outcomes and boosts engagement in any classroom.
Learn more
EdTech 2024
Kahoot! is ranked top among classroom response & assessment tools in the latest EdTech Top 40 report, issued by Instructure’s Learn Platform.
Learn more
Guides and resources for schools
Get started with Kahoot! EDU for Schools
Welcome aboard! You’ll be making and playing awesome learning games in no time – just follow the step-by-step instructions in this guide.
Learn more
Get started with Kahoot! EDU for Higher Ed
Explore how to use more advanced features designed for Higher Ed institutions, professors, admins, educators and student
Learn more
Teacher Talks webinars
Earn PD credits with our monthly webinars, where teachers from our Kahoot! community share advice on using Kahoot! in their teaching.
Learn more
Kahoot! Teacher Hacks
Learn tips and tricks from fellow teachers from across the globe!
Through quick 30 second videos enjoy bitesized hacks for your classroom.
Learn more
Get Kahoot! certified and earn PD credits
Learn how you can save time, simplify lesson planning, and create engaging learning experiences your students will love!
The program is entirely free.
Learn more
Kahoot! learning experience channel
Explore the Kahoot! learning experience with free mini courses covering key features and how to bring more value and fun to your classroom. Each takes less than 10 minutes.
Learn more
Best practices for content creation
Here, you’ll find helpful guidelines to support you in creating high-quality content that can be discovered by millions of educators, students, and business users worldwide.
Learn more
Become a Kahoot! verified creator
Join the Kahoot! Community by becoming a verified creator. Your content will help millions of educators and students worldwide.
Learn more
Become a Kahoot! ambassador
Connect, host, and represent the Kahoot! community. Support product development, beta testing, and curriculum creation with like-minded educators from around the world.
Learn more
Teacher stories
Discover inspiring stories from educators in our Teacher Takeover blog series. Learn how teachers around the world are using Kahoot! in creative ways to engage their students and enhance learning.
Learn more
Accessible learning
At Kahoot!, we believe that education should be accessible to everyone and that inclusion is essential in all aspects of education. When education is inclusive, everyone benefits.
Learn more
Special education with Kahoot!
We believe that education should be accessible to everyone, and inclusion is essential in every learning environment. Get inspired by some stories from special education classrooms.
Learn more
Guides and resources for business
Guide for team members
Learn how to get started with Kahoot!, how to create and play kahoots, and access reporting.
Learn more
Guide for admins
Learn how to get your business set up with Kahoot!, invite other team members and collaborate on learning content.
Learn more
Superstar Presenter Spotlight
Dive into our 2024 Superstar Presenter Spotlight to learn more about challenges and opportunities for professionals when delivering presentations.
Learn more
Webinars and events
Webinars for education
We’re running free themed webinars for education. Sign up for one of the upcoming webinars, or view recordings of our previous ones.
Learn more
Webinars for business
We’re running free themed webinars for business. Sign up for one of the upcoming webinars, or view recordings of our previous ones.
Learn more

FAQs
What is the Kahoot! community?
The Kahoot! community is an international network and knowledge platform for all creators, learners and learning providers. It allows millions to connect with each other globally, it provides access to unique learning content created by trusted creators and publishers.
What is a Kahoot! verified profile?
A verified profile lets you connect with other like-minded individuals around the world and share your awesome learning content with millions! When you join the Kahoot! community, you gain access to your own verified profile where you can not only connect and share content, but also broadcast social media profiles, websites and updates with a global community. The Kahoot! community team promotes verified profiles to our community of millions on the Kahoot! website and app. See verified educator profiles by Brendan Timlin , Norman Fandel and Linda Sees for inspiration!
Do I need to be a verified creator to be able to sell my resources on the K! marketplace?
Yes the first step to selling your content is to become a verified creator. However you also need to be on an individual paid plan in order to sell content. For more information on the marketplace see here.
Do I need to be on a paid plan to be able to join the community?
Nope! Anyone can join whether you are on a free plan, school plan, family plan or business plan. We are open to everyone!
Can I join the community if I am not a teacher?
Absolutely! We have business users, students, social users and much more!
Can I be both a verified creator and a Kahoot! ambassador?
You certainly can! We encourage you to apply for both because to be an ambassador you need to be able to demonstrate knowledge of the platform, and the best way to do this is through your verified profile.
Will I be able to connect with the other K! community members?
Absolutely, you can follow other verified profiles and you can in fact broadcast messages to your followers on the platform. You can join our newsletter to get exclusive updates, meet people at our events and join our private facebook group for verified creators.

Accessibility at Kahoot!
At Kahoot!, we believe that learning should include everyone and our mission is to make learning accessible to anyone, regardless of ability, device, or internet access.
We strive to meet WCAG 2.2 Level AA.
Explore our inclusion and accessibility policy
Inclusion is one of our core values. From game experiences that reduce stress and anxiety to features that support different learning styles, Kahoot! is built with everyone in mind.
Educators can also customize their kahoots to be even more inclusive — adjusting pacing, question types, and settings to suit every learner in the room. When education is truly inclusive, everyone benefits.
Kahoot! Accessibility Conformance Report (ACR)
Our Accessibility Conformance Report (ACR) helps schools, institutions, and businesses understand how Kahoot! supports global accessibility standards in conformance with WCAG 2.2 Level AA.
Download the reports for our instructor and participant experiences below:
For instructors For participants
Accessibility features built for every learner
Read aloud
Questions and answers are read aloud via iOS and Android apps, supporting students with reading difficulties or visual impairments.
High contrast mode
A high-contrast theme improves readability for users with mild to moderate visual impairments, making content easier to see and engage with.
Questions on player devices
Show questions directly on each student’s device — ideal for those using assistive technologies or who need to be closer to the screen.
Screen reader compatibility
Compatible with common screen readers on student devices, reading questions and answers aloud for most question types. Drop Pin and Pin answers are visual and not supported.
Extended time & no-timer options
Remove or extend time limits on questions to reduce pressure and give every student the time they need to respond confidently.
Dynamic font sizes
Text scales up to 200% via browser zoom and dynamically adjusts font sizes on mobile for improved readability across all devices.
Keyboard navigation
Full keyboard navigation support using Alt, Enter, and Spacebar, ensuring Kahoot! is usable without a mouse or touchscreen.
Multi-modal question types
Multiple-choice, open-ended, image or GIF answers, and video questions give educators flexibility to support reading challenges and diverse learning needs.
Accuracy mode
Puts the spotlight on understanding over speed, giving every learner equal time to answer and rewarding accuracy — reducing pressure and promoting fairness.
Game experiences designed for every classroom
We offer a range of game experiences for different learning styles and classroom environments. Accuracy Mode and Kahootopia! are built with accessibility in mind — reducing stress, rewarding accuracy over speed, and supporting collaborative play.
Accuracy mode
Rewards correct answers over speed, reducing pressure, and creating a fairer experience for all learners.
Kahootopia!
Every student’s contribution builds a shared class achievement, celebrating learning as a community.
Frequently Asked Questions
Does Kahoot! meet accessibility standards?
Kahoot! strives to meet WCAG 2.2 Level AA. We have two Accessibility Conformance Reports (ACR) covering the instructor and participant experience, each available to download as a PDF or view in your browser:
Instructor experience — PDF | View in browser
Participant experience — PDF | View in browser
Is Kahoot! compatible with screen readers?
Yes. Kahoot! is compatible with common screen readers on both teaching and student devices, including iOS and Android. Questions and answers can also be read aloud through the Read Aloud feature in the Kahoot! mobile app.
Can I remove the timer for students who need more time?
Yes. Educators can remove timers entirely or extend time limits on individual questions, giving every student the time they need to respond without pressure.
Where can I find Kahoot!'s inclusion and accessibility policy?
Our full inclusion and accessibility policy is available on the Kahoot! Trust Center, and you can also explore our accessibility settings.
How do I enable accessibility features in Kahoot!?
Accessibility features such as high contrast mode, read aloud, and questions on player devices can be found in your game settings before launching a kahoot. Visit our accessibility settings page for a full step-by-step guide.
Need more help? Visit our Support Center or Trust Center for full accessibility documentation.

How Kahoot!+ supports academic success in higher education
Kahoot!+ is far more than just an interactive quiz platform. In higher education, it empowers instructors to meet every student’s needs in both in-person and hybrid sessions—streamlining planning, boosting student engagement, and supporting summative and formative assessment with the Kahoot! Learning Experience.
Empower students to be active learners
Transform passive listeners into active participants. Get real-time feedback and foster participation during lectures with polls, word clouds, brainstorms, and Q&A—empowering students to ask meaningful questions.
Get started
Encourage students to learn independently
Help students to take charge of their learning with interactive self-study tools like flashcards and self-paced quizzes, making studying flexible and effective—all on their own terms.
Get started
Review understanding of key concepts
Assess students’ understanding and introduce topics across any discipline using assessment questions. Real-time reports help refine teaching strategies and support exam preparation.
Get started
Looking to equip more educators at your institution with engaging instructional tools?
Get in touch to learn more about our Kahoot! EDU offering.
Book an intro
Discover tools to engage students, foster collaboration, and enhance academic success with Kahoot!+
Simplify lecture preparation with AI-powered tools
Instantly create kahoots from existing material with the AI generator. Turn handwritten notes, PDFs, Wikipedia articles, URLS, or slides into lectures. This active learning technology also generates flashcards and self-study activities to support test prep and independent learning.
Get started
Assess learning and spark discussion with interactive questions
Facilitate conversation with interactive question types—crafted to spark active learning, critical thinking, and knowledge retention. Blend quizzes, polls, and puzzles to reinforce understanding, encourage thoughtful debate, and inspire learners to voice their opinions.
Get started
Encourage deeper thinking with accuracy and confidence modes
Accuracy mode rewards thoughtful analysis over quick responses—only correct answers count, so students can pause and apply critical thinking. With confidence mode, students indicate how confident they are with each answer, gaining extra points for being confidently correct, or losing them if they’re wrong. It’s a safe‑to‑fail way to spot knowledge gaps and misconceptions.
Get started
Create dynamic learning experiences with lecture mode
Seamlessly sync your slides with your kahoots and deliver your content with lecture mode, the ultimate lecture engagement tool. Easily decide when to pause for a poll, spark debate with a word cloud, or introduce a competitive quiz moment, all aligned with your teaching style. Encourage students to ask questions and vote for the ones they find most relevant using Q&A upvoting, helping you quickly identify the areas where they need the most support.
Get started
Integrate with leading pedagogical tools
Whether you’re looking to integrate Kahoot! into your LMS, spark discussions in virtual lectures with Zoom integration, or maximize engagement with Google Classroom, Kahoot! provides connectivity with a variety of educational tools to enhance learning experiences.
Get started
Turn engagement into insight with reporting tools
Access actionable insights into student engagement, academic progress, and areas for improvement—ensuring course learning outcomes are met and helping you adapt your teaching strategies based on clear data. Support every student’s academic journey with data-driven decisions.
Get started

FAQs
How does Kahoot! work in a university classroom?
Kahoot! transforms traditional lectures into active learning experiences. Professors can seamlessly integrate live polls, quizzes, and open-ended questions into their presentations, allowing students to participate in real time using their own devices. This instant feedback loop helps assess comprehension, spark meaningful debates, and keep large lecture halls fully engaged from start to finish.
Does Kahoot! integrate with Canvas, Blackboard, or Moodle?
Yes, Kahoot! is designed to fit smoothly into your existing higher education ecosystem. We provide connectivity with major Learning Management Systems and educational tools, making it easy to share assignments, host sessions, and streamline your lesson preparation without leaving your preferred platforms.
Is Kahoot! free for college professors?
Yes, we offer Kahoot! Go, a free plan for Higher Ed educators. It provides the essential tools for creating engaging lectures, motivating students, and tracking progress. To access advanced capabilities such as AI quiz generation, seamless slide integrations with lecture mode, comprehensive reporting, and higher student participant limits, professors can upgrade to our Kahoot!+ plans.
How many students can join a Kahoot! session at once?
Participant limits scale with your subscription level to accommodate classes of any size. You can check all details here.
Can students use Kahoot! for self-study?
Absolutely. Kahoot! empowers college students to take charge of their own learning outside of scheduled class time. Professors can assign self-paced quizzes for homework, while students can leverage AI tools to generate interactive flashcards and practice materials to confidently prepare for upcoming exams.
Does Kahoot! work with Zoom for online and hybrid classes?
Yes, Kahoot! is built to support flexible pedagogical environments. Our seamless Zoom integration allows you to host interactive sessions directly within your virtual meetings, ensuring you can foster active participation and maintain a strong connection with students, whether they are on campus or joining remotely.
What is the difference between Kahoot!+ Bronze, Silver, and Gold for higher education?
Each tier is designed to scale with your instructional goals. Bronze covers everyday learning with basic review tools and quick knowledge checks. Silver introduces powerful presentation features like lecture mode and advanced question types to drive deeper participation. Gold unlocks our total learning experience, providing the full AI-powered toolkit, comprehensive tracking reports, and advanced discussion formats to support deep learning mastery across larger student groups. You can check all details for each plan here.

Frequently asked questions
Is the pricing listed per team or per teacher?
Unless specified otherwise in a special offer or promotion, price is listed per teacher.
Can someone else pay for my subscription?
You can request a Kahoot!+ Voucher. All you need to do is generate a link and share it with someone who is eager to help. This allows them to pay for Kahoot! subscription of your selection. You can generate the link in the Billing tab of your account settings.
Is it annual billing?
The prices displayed on our website include both the annual cost and the monthly breakdown of the annual subscription. However, all these plans are billed upfront for the entire year. Currently, there is no option for monthly billing for any of our educational plans.
I’d like to collaborate with my colleagues. Is there a team plan available?
Kahoot! EDU is our recommended plan for teams and education institutions.
Where can I find Kahoot!’s W-9 form?
Download our signed W-8-BEN-E form here.
Does Kahoot! provide a sole source letter?
Please send us a request to schools@kahoot.com.
How do I get a public profile?
If you have a Kahoot!+ Silver subscription, just reach out to us at schools@kahoot.com and we’ll set you up.
Can I get a site license?
Absolutely! At a discounted price, admins can empower and connect educators across a campus or district with versatile tools for blended and distance learning. Learn more about a Kahoot! EDU site license and request a personalized quote here.
Can I use Kahoot! in languages other than English?
Yes! You can write questions and answers in any language to create a native language experience for participants. You can also play, create and host kahoots, view reports and more in Spanish, Brazilian Portuguese, French, German, Italian, Japanese, Dutch, Polish, Turkish, Norwegian, Swedish, Danish, Arabic, Ukrainian, Korean, Indonesian, Simplified Chinese, Traditional Chinese, Thai and Vietnamese. More languages are coming soon to our apps and website!
I can't find my current plan in the offer.
Do you have an active higher ed subscription that isn’t visible on the pricing pages? Check the features of higher ed legacy plans here.

Got a question? Find your answer!
How can I support my child’s learning using Kahoot!?
For children aged 2-7:
Kahoot! Kids is tailored specifically for young children, typically in the early elementary age range. It is designed to provide a safe and engaging learning environment where kids can play learning apps and quizzes that cover various subjects such as math, reading, and social-emotinal learning.
Asa parent you can set up and manage accounts for your children, track their progress, and control the content they can access with personalized learning paths and playlists. This helps ensure that kids are engaging with age-appropriate material. Your children can play individually or in groups, which can encourage collaboration and healthy competition.
For children aged 8-16:
For older children and students, we recommend using the Kahoot! app which offers engaging learning experiences with multiple games and study modes to make learning at home enjoyable and effective. With Kahoot!+ for young students subscription, they will unlock exclusive reward, access to math games and award-winning learning apps. It supports learning across core subjects and school topics, helping them thrive in school!
Both Kahoot! Kids and Kahoot!+ for young students are included in Kahoot!+ for personal users. For pricing details, please visit Kahoot!+ for personal use.
How do I create a child profile?
With your adult account, you have the ability to create, edit, and delete child profiles linked to your account. This allows you to ensure a safe and age-appropriate experience tailored to each child’s needs.
Get started by downloading the Kahoot! app:
After accessing the Kahoot! app, go to the home screen.
Click on your profile picture in the upper left corner.
Select “Add child”.
Provide the child’s age.
Set a nickname.
Select the avatar.
Done!
Then, download the Kahoot! app (for children above 8) or the Kahoot! Kids app (for children below 8) to your child’s device.
You can also create a child profile in the Kahoot! Kids app:
In the Kahoot! Kids app, tap on the Parents button in the top left corner.
Provide your date of birth to confirm that you’re an adult.
Go to the My Kids section.
Select the profile.
Tap Edit Profile or Delete Profile.
How to access Kahoot! Kids?
Kahoot! Kids stand-alone app is available on iOS and Android devices.
Go to the app store on your mobile device and search for Kahoot! Kids. Alternatively, tap the link:
Kahoot! Kids on iOS
Kahoot! Kids on Android
Download and launch the app.
Within Kahoot! App:
Open the Kahoot! app and login or sign up.
Tap on the avatar icon in the top left corner of the screen to open your profile.
On the top of the screen, you’ll see the existing profiles. Tap on the Child profile or one of the previously created child profiles to open a child profile’s space in Kahoot! app.
💡 Here our younger users can create their own kahoots, discover child-friendly ready-made quizzes, and join games.
Go to the Home tab.
Tab on the icon in the top right corner to open Kahoot! Kids.
Kahoot! Kids is also included in Kahoot!+ for personal users. For more information on pricing, check out Kahoot!+ for personal use.
What learning apps and games suit my child?
From 4 to 8 years old
Learning apps:
Kahoot! Learn to read by Poio
Poio is an immersive game designed so that children learn through their own exploration, triggering their curiosity and motivation to practice without the need for support. It adapts to each child’s level of skill, so that all children can enjoy learning how to read.
Kahoot! Numbers by DragonBox
Easily learn what numbers are, how they work, and what you can do with them. With four innovative activities, learning fluidity and flexibility with numbers has never been easier!
Kahoot! Big Numbers by DragonBox
Learn big numbers, long addition, and subtraction in DragonBox Big Numbers! Players do 4000 operations to gather, trade, and unlock new worlds.
Kahoot! Algebra 1 by DragonBox
An intuitive and engaging game that teaches children as young as 5 the process needed to solve basic linear equations.
Kahoot! Learn Chess by DragonBox
An epic learning adventure for anyone who wants to learn how to play chess. Join grandmaster Max on her adventure to solve puzzles and beat bosses, and become a grandmaster yourself!
From 8 and up
Kahoot! Multiplication by DragonBox
Turn the chore of memorizing the times tables into an awesome experience. 20+ wacky and exciting mini games keep kids practicing until they can recite the times tables with ease.
Kahoot! Algebra 2 by DragonBox
Building on the teachings of Algebra 1, this app takes it to the next level with more advanced algebra and mathematic topics.
Kahoot! Geometry by DragonBox
Challenge yourself to save Euclid’s Island while learning the properties, definitions, and relations of geometric shapes through Euclidian proofs.
Math games by DragonBox (in Kahoot! app only):
Kahoot! Duck Duck Fractions and Snoopy Fractions (iOS only)
Explore fractions in innovative ways. Similar to traditional kahoots, the Kahoot! Duck Duck Fractions is hosted on a big screen and students join with a PIN code. Kahoot! Duck Duck Fractions lets students manipulate fractions to better feel and explore their meaning. The Snoopy Fractions game mode follows the same logic as the Kahoot! Duck-Duck Fractions mode.
Kahoot! Pop-Pop Multiplications
Offers new ways for learners to explore math topics and expand their skills through strategic thinking. Similar to traditional kahoots, Pop-Pop Multiplications allows you to host on a big screen and players can join with a unique PIN code. Kahoot! Pop-Pop Multiplications does not use a quiz system to transfer knowledge but adapts specific and unique interaction-based gameplay on multiplication. You can also play it solo the in Kahoot! App.
How to play kahoot?
Start a live game.
The host’s screen will display available game modes. Click options on the carousel at the bottom of the screen to see short descriptions. Choose the one that best matches the gameplay experience you want to provide your audience.
Classic
Use classic mode if you want every player to play individually.
Team mode
Use team mode if you want players to form teams. You can read more about Team Mode here.
Other game modes
Kahoot! game modes empower every aspect of dynamics between players, from lesson or meeting planning to delivery, through to assessment and reporting. Read more:
Tallest tower
Color kingdoms
Treasure trove
Submarine squad
Chill art
Before you choose the game mode, in the lobby when waiting for the players and in-between questions, you can go to a list of game options that can be accessed via the gear wheel icon on the bottom right corner of the host’s screen.
You can modify some of those settings during the game, in between questions. Your settings will be saved for next time. You can read more about settings here.
How to make a kahoot?
Log in to your account.
Click the Create button on the right side of the top navigation bar.
Select the Kahoot option from the drop-down menu to make a kahoot.
Once you’ve started making a kahoot, you can navigate between questions and add new ones via the left-hand sidebar. You can create new questions from scratch, copy them from other kahoots, import slides from a presentation, or import quiz questions from a spreadsheet.
Your kahoot creator shows different features depending on the current workspace.
How to add questions
When making a kahoot, click the Add question button on the left-side panel below the questions you already added.
Choose the question type.
Type in the question. The questions from the Test knowledge section require you to provide the answers and mark the correct response (if applicable for the question type you selected).
🖌️ If you forget about an element of a question or you exceeded the character limit, an exclamation mark will appear on the left-side panel once you leave the question.
🖌️ Features marked with a star are not included in your current plan. If you use them, you’ll be asked to upgrade to share, host, and assign your game. Read more about saving games.
🖌️ You can add up to 100 questions per kahoot.
🖌️ Read more: Question types
How to move, duplicate, or delete questions
Click on a question on the left-side panel.
Drag a question vertically in the panel to place it above or below another question.
Click the duplication icon to duplicate a question.
Click the trash bin icon to delete a question.
How to search for kahoots?
Over 30 million public kahoots have been created by our team, partners, and community. Whether you teach 1st grade, college, or train new hires, you can likely find pre-made games that suit your needs.
To get started, log in to your Kahoot! account and type in the relevant phrase in the search bar at the top of the screen or go to the Discover page via the left-side panel.
How to search:
You can use the search bar near the top of the page. Type a keyword for a list of matching kahoots. Or to see a list of kahoots someone’s made, type in their username.
Child account holders can search only the free pre-selected kahoots that we’ve vetted as both appropriate and awesome. The filters are not available.
You can only see public kahoots. Private kahoots must be shared with you and will be found under Shared with you on your Library page.
Keyword and username terms are not case-sensitive.
Filters – You can filter results by type of free and premium offer, common subjects, as well as grade level, creator’s role, and language. Additionally, you can choose if you want to display individual kahoots, channels, or courses.
Sort by – Three sorting options let you prioritize what is shown at the top:
Most Relevant – what we think is an ideal mix of factors including popularity and quality.
Most Played – those with the most plays.
Highest Quality – this considers many factors (for example, average playtime and how many times each kahoot was favorited).
💡 TIP 💡
The operator * searches an unlimited number of characters in place of the ” * ” symbol (Ex: *ath will find “math” as well as “telepath” and “wreath”).
How to find paid and free content
Kahoot!’s Discover page offers both free and paid content. Among paid content, we distinguish Kahoot! Marketplace and Kahoot!+ AccessPass subscriptions.
You can filter the free content, Kahoot!+ AccessPass and Kahoot! Marketplace content through the panel on the left side of the results page.
When navigating our Discover page and browsing through your search results, pay attention to the labels placed at the bottom of the covers of kahoots and courses.
If the label says Free, the content is free.
If the label says K!+ AccessPass, the content is available for Kahoot!+ AccessPass subscribers.
If the label says Purchase, the content can be purchased on our marketplace and you’ll see the price after clicking on it.

Plans & Pricing
Learn a new language
Want to learn a new language? It only takes 5 minutes per day with the Drops language learning app!
Join more than 50 million people who are already learning
Get started for free
How Drops makes language learning awesome
Captivating game-based learning for all ages
Designed as a captivating game, Drops is easy to use for learners of all ages and backgrounds. All you need to get started is a mobile device! Through a series of games and interactive practice, you’re introduced to thousands of new words in a variety of categories, such as Travel Talk, Fun and Recreation, Nature and Animals, and more.
Get going in just 5 minutes a day
With Drops, you’re not committed to long, intensive learning sessions but can instead enjoy quick, fun, and focused practice. Just 5 minutes a day is enough! Get a Premium subscription to practice even more.
Choose from 50 languages
Languages supported by Drops range from popular picks such as Spanish, Portuguese and Japanese, to Polynesian languages and rare languages such as Ainu. See a full list of languages on Drops language learning.
Turn language learning into a daily habit
Drops will help you turn language learning into a sustainable daily habit. Since it only requires 5 minutes a day to practice, it’s easy to keep up and make progress. And even if you miss a day, don’t worry – you won’t lose your score streak.
Personalized learning journey
After you’ve learned 50 words, you can then visit the Review Dojo, a special section in Drops designed to reinforce knowledge. There, you can test what you’ve learned with our spaced-repetition algorithm. Based on your performance, it will flag which specific words need some more practice.
Learn and compete in multiplayer mode
Stay motivated by competing against language learners from around the world in multiplayer mode. You’ll play your favorite topic-based learning games but with some added competition to maximize your focus and fun.
Language learning made easy
4.8 out of 5
300,000+ reviews

Download the app

FAQ
What is Drops by Kahoot!?
Drops is a language-learning app designed to make learning new languages fast, fun, and effective by turning it into a game! New words and phrases are read aloud and matched with images depicting the word’s meaning. During learning sessions, you are prompted to recall each word and its matching image. Through visual learning, repetition, a variety of game modes, and a unique achievement system, all it takes is 5 minutes per day to learn any of the 50+ languages available on the Drops app!
How does Drops help me learn languages?
As a part of the Kahoot! family, Drops uses engaging gamified learning strategies to help you build effective habits. Drops encourages consistent practice and motivation through achievements earned by mastering topics, conquering challenges, and maintaining your daily streak. You can choose how you want to learn with a number of game modes suited to any learning style. In addition to mastering new terms in a topic and completing challenges, you can test your knowledge in quiz mode or start a guided learning journey with language voyage. You can even practice writing in new scripts by tracing letters right on your screen!
Which languages are on Drops?
Drops has over 50 languages to choose from! That’s more than the other popular language learning apps and we’re always working on adding even more! Start your language learning journey with any of these languages available in our app today: 1. Ainu 2. Arabic 3. American Sign Language 4. Bosnian 5. Catalan 6. Danish 7. German 8. Greek 9. British English 10. American English 11. Esperanto 12. Castilian Spanish 13. Mexican Spanish 14. Estonian 15. Persian 16. Finnish 17. French 18. Irish 19. Galician 20. Hebrew 21. Hindi 22. Hawaiian 23. Croatian 24. Hungarian 25. Icelandic 26. Indonesian 27. Igbo 28. Italian 29. Japanese 30. Korean 31. Maori 32. Dutch 33. Norwegian 34. Polish 35. Brazilian Portuguese 36. European Portuguese 37. Romanian 38. Russian 39. Sanskrit 40. Samoan 41. Serbian 42. Swedish 43. Swahili 44. Thai 45. Tagalog 46. Turkish 47. Ukrainian 48. Vietnamese 49. Yoruba 50. Mandarin Chinese 51. Cantonese Chinese
Is Drops app free?
You can learn online with all of Drops’ language-learning features for free for up to 5 minutes per day! Upgrading to Drops Premium will unlock unlimited ad-free learning time, offline learning, our entire library of content, and exclusive gameplay.

Frequently asked questions
Can I play Kahoot! for free with my friends and family?
Yes, you can host a kahoot for free with up to 10 players. If you want to play with more players, check our Kahoot! plans above.
Do players need a paid subscription?
No, players don’t need a subscription to play your kahoots, and can play without a Kahoot! account.
Where can I read more about the learning apps included in Kahoot!+ Bronze, Silver and Gold for personal users?
Visit these pages to find more information about Kahoot! DragonBox and Poio apps:
DragonBox math and chess apps
Poio learn to read app
How can I redeem my access to the learning apps included in Kahoot!+ Bronze, Silver and Gold for personal users?
To unlock your unlimited access to Kahoot! Learn to Read by Poio, Kahoot! Numbers by DragonBox, Kahoot! Big Numbers by DragonBox, Kahoot! Algebra by DragonBox, Kahoot! Algebra 2 by DragonBox, Kahoot! Geometry by DragonBox, Kahoot! Learn Chess by DragonBox, and Kahoot! Kids, download them from the App Store or Google Play and use your Kahoot! credentials to log in.
Alternatively, you can download all apps through the Kahoot! app. Open the app, log in to your account with Kahoot!+ Bronze, Silver or Gold subscription, and tap Download now from your home screen.
Download the Kahoot! app now for Android or iOS.
I am planning a social activity at work. What plan should I choose?
Please choose one of our plans for businesses.
Can I play Kahoot! over video?
Yes, you can host a live kahoot via Skype, Google Meet, Microsoft Teams, Zoom, SharePlay or another video conferencing tool. Share your screen and the computer sound, while players join remotely with the game PIN from their devices.
Can I use Kahoot! in languages other than English?
Yes! You can write questions and answers in any language to create a native language experience for participants. You can also play, create and host kahoots, view reports, and more in Spanish, French, Brazilian Portuguese, German, Italian, Norwegian, Swedish, Danish, Dutch, Turkish, Polish, Japanese, Arabic, Ukrainian, Korean, Indonesian, Simplified Chinese, Traditional Chinese, Thai and Vietnamese. Kahoot! is more accessible than ever!
How can I find ready-to-play kahoots for children?
Easily, with Kahoot! Kids! – a new app experience for the youngest learners. Kahoot! Kids is unlocked with a Kahoot!+ Bronze, Silver or Gold for personal users subscription. When you upgrade, you’ll not only unlock Kahoot! Kids, but 8 of Kahoot!’s award-winning learning apps and premium features to create family-friendly kahoots, with more to come soon!
Is there a monthly option?
Yes. Our personal plans can be billed monthly. In order to view and purchase the monthly offer, switch the toggle right below the price cards to the Monthly option.
How can I share learning apps on multiple devices?
If you purchase a Kahoot! subscription that includes access to the learning apps, you can create up to 6 profiles on your account. You will need to use the same login details for each profile. You can read more about this here.
Do my kids need an account to get access to the learning apps?
No, you can create a profile for your kids within your account. You will need to use the same login details for each profile.
Do I get access to pre-made premium content (AccessPass) with my subscription?
Access to the pre-made premium content (AccessPass) is included with the Kahoot!+ Gold annual subscriptions. If you purchased before that date or chose a different plan, your subscription does not include the AccessPass content.
On this pricing page you can see and buy feature subscriptions.To purchase the content subscription AcessPass go here. You can have both feature and content subscription at the same time. Read more about feature and content subscriptions
I can't find my current plan in the offer.
Do you have an active personal subscription that isn’t visible on the pricing pages? Check the features of legacy plans for personal use.
Where can I find games for my social event?
You can search through thousands of ready-to-play kahoots and courses on the Discover page. Filters in the search bar will help you find the perfect game for your event! If you’re looking for higher-quality premium content, you can check out our Marketplace.
Got a question about Kahoot! for personal use?
Contact our support team by submitting your request via this form and we’ll get back to you as soon as possible!

Get it all in Kahoot! One
Kahoot! One provides the ultimate learning experience by combining all our features, apps, and premium content into one complete solution. With up to five individual accounts, your entire household can host great get-togethers, engage with their community, or learn on their own. More awesome learning, at even greater value.
Get started
Experience everything on Kahoot!
Bring events, get-togethers, and study sessions to life
Unleash your creativity with all question types and the power of AI. Transform websites, PDFs, and more into kahoots. Plus, turn handwritten notes into study sets.
Make learning fun with personalized experiences
Gain full access to the best premium content, rewards, and skins that Kahoot! has to offer. With our Season, Access, and Experience Passes, you unlock everything.
Create engaging learning opportunities for the family
Give family members their own place to learn and grow, all connected in a shared family space. Learn, celebrate, and unlock rewards together with Super Kahootopia.
Learn new languages and unlock all our apps for kids

Your questions answered
Can I use Kahoot! One on multiple devices?
Yes, you can access your account with your Kahoot! One subscription on multiple devices. However, there are specific features that can only be used on the app or website.
How does a family account work?
A family account with Kahoot! One allows up to 5 individual accounts for family members at the same home address. These accounts can share a common family space where they can create and share kahoots.
Do Kahoot! One plans or include parental controls to manage screen time or restrict access?
Kahoot! One does not currently include parental controls to manage screen time or restrict access to specific content.
Who is Kahoot! One for?
Kahoot! One is perfect for families with members of all ages, and groups of friends or housemates. Meanwhile, Kahoot! One Max is perfect for larger clubs, communities, and those looking to sell content on our marketplace.
Can I subscribe as a teacher or business user?
Teachers and educators can find everything they need within Kahoot! EDU, built specifically to increase student engagement, unlock advanced admin tools, and bring the Kahoot! experience to every physical and virtual classroom. Companies and business users should explore our dedicated work plans, Kahoot! 360, for individuals, teams, enterprise, or one-time events.
Can I purchase the features, apps, and content in Kahoot! One separately?
Some features available on Kahoot! One can be purchased separately, but Kahoot! One offers much better value than individual purchases and subscriptions.
How do I get the Drops language learning app with Kahoot! One?
When you subscribe to Kahoot! One or One Max, you’ll receive a welcome email explaining how to get Drops Premium.

Got questions? Get answers!
Can I join the student community and become a Kahoot! student ambassador?
Absolutely! We have a K!student ambassador program where you can apply for free, complete fun missions on social media, and win cool K! swag and free access to all the premium features. Join us!
Can Kahoot! be used for test prep?
Our online self-study tools for students are perfect for test preparation. You can use flashcards or learn mode to learn new content and then quiz yourself under timed conditions to see if you’re exam-ready!
How do I create flashcards on Kahoot!?
Simply create your kahoot with your chosen questions and answers, choose to play in solo mode, and then select flashcards.
What study tools are available in the Kahoot! App?
The Kahoot! app has many online study tools for students, including flashcards, practice activities, tests, presentation tools, and ready-made learning content.
Does Kahoot! offer AI-powered online study tools for students?
Many of our study tools use AI to help you generate effective learning materials as quickly as possible. You can also use AI to generate questions for existing kahoots or images to make your materials more engaging.
How can I study effectively with Kahoot!?
We recommend using our AI Generator to instantly create study sets from class materials, trusted online sources, or handwritten notes. Then, choose to learn independently or in a group, and make use of our practice activities, flashcards, and tests.
How do study groups work in Kahoot!?
First, you need to make a study group by going to your library, selecting groups, and choosing to create a new group. Then you simply find the kahoot you want to study with and select the study group game mode.
Is Kahoot! free for students to use for studying?
The basic Kahoot! experience is free to try, but you’ll find the best online study tools for students in our paid plans, Kahoot!+ Bronze, Kahoot!+ Silver, and Kahoot!+ Gold.
What subjects are covered by Kahoot! quizzes?
You’ll find ready-made content on almost every subject. If you can’t find what you’re looking for, you can instantly generate a quiz from any URL, Wikipedia article, PDFs, or even your hand-written notes.
Can Kahoot! be used for test preparation in college?
Kahoot! offers study tools for college students, study tools for high school students, and even study tools for young children on Kahoot! Kids.
Can Kahoot! help me with homework?
Kahoot! is a very effective homework helper, especially for learning new material or preparing class presentations. You can upload your homework and practice answering questions in fun and interactive formats. For presentations, you can sync your Google Slides or PowerPoints and enhance them with points and podiums to keep your class engaged.

Frequently asked questions
Are there ready-made flashcards available on Kahoot?
There are millions of ready-made study sets on create.kahoot.it/discover. And if you go in the app, you can easily search for study sets, specifically made for studying with flashcards! You will easily find them by going to “ways to play”, then press “flashcards”.
Are Kahoot’s online flashcards free?
You can create unlimited free online flashcards. However, there are som premium features available for additional functionality. Such as premium ready-made content, and the PDF-to-kahoot generator and question generator enhanced by AI, these are availiable with a Kahoot!+ Gold plan.
How can Kahoot’s flashcards for studying help me to improve my grades?
Using Kahoot’s flashcards for studying can be beneficial for improving grades in several ways:
Interactive Learning: Kahoot’s flashcards are designed to be interactive and engaging. They incorporate elements of visuals, audio, and videos, making studying more enjoyable and effective.
Active Recall: Flashcards are an excellent tool for active recall, a proven study technique where you actively retrieve information from memory. By regularly testing yourself on the flashcards, you reinforce your memory and understanding of the material. Set study goals in the app to make sure you are going back each day!
Repetition and Review: Flashcards allow you to review information repeatedly, helping to reinforce learning and increase retention over time.
Customization: Kahoot’s flashcards allow you to create your own sets, which means you can tailor them to your specific study needs and focus on the material that you find most challenging.
Accessibility: Kahoot’s flashcards are accessible on desktop and via mobile devices, allowing you to study anytime, anywhere. This flexibility makes it easier to fit study sessions into your schedule, leading to more consistent and effective studying.
How do I create my own flashcards using the Kahoot’s AI flashcard generator?
Log in to your Kahoot!+ Gold account. Don’t have one? No worries, signing up is quick.
Go to the AI PDF-to-Kahoot generator, click Create, and pick Question Generator.
Upload your PDF, and let the magic happen.
Personalize your questions. After the AI does the hard work, choose the best questions from the suggestions, add images, and adjust answers to make your Kahoot unique.
Save and share your Kahoot. Once you’re happy, save it and open it in the Kahoot! app to study with flashcards for your exams.
Can I use Kahoot’s flashcard creator to make flashcards from PDF?
Absolutely! The AI extracts questions and answers from your PDFs to make studying complex topics easier. Upload your PDF, and customize the questions, then study with flashcards using Kahoot! app.
Can I use the flashcard maker for business purposes?
Yes, the flashcard maker is great for business and team training. It’s an effective way to boost learning on a larger scale, whether live or self-paced. Find more information about Kahoot! At work here!
Are there ready-made flashcards available on Kahoot?
Definitely! There are millions of ready-made study sets on Kahoot! To find them, open the app, click “Discover more ways to play,” then “Flashcards.” You can search by topic to find the perfect flashcards for your needs!

FAQs
Can Kahoot! be used for test prep?
Our online self-study tools for students are perfect for test preparation. You can use flashcards or learn mode to learn new content and then quiz yourself under timed conditions to see if you’re exam-ready!
How do I create flashcards on Kahoot!?
Simply create your kahoot with your chosen questions and answers, choose to play in solo mode, and then select flashcards.
What study tools are available in the Kahoot! App?
The Kahoot! app has many online study tools for students, including flashcards, practice activities, tests, presentation tools, and ready-made learning content.
Does Kahoot! offer AI-powered online study tools for students?
Many of our study tools use AI to help you generate effective learning materials as quickly as possible. You can also use AI to generate questions for existing kahoots or images to make your materials more engaging.
How can I study effectively with Kahoot!?
We recommend using our AI Generator to instantly create study sets from class materials, trusted online sources, or handwritten notes. Then, choose to learn independently or in a group, and make use of our practice activities, flashcards, and tests.
How do study groups work in Kahoot!?
First, you need to make a study group by going to your library, selecting groups, and choosing to create a new group. Then you simply find the kahoot you want to study with and select the study group game mode.
Is Kahoot! free for students to use for studying?
The basic Kahoot! experience is free to try, but you’ll find the best online study tools for students in our paid plans, Kahoot!+ Bronze, Kahoot!+ Silver, and Kahoot!+ Gold.
What subjects are covered by Kahoot! quizzes?
You’ll find ready-made content on almost every subject. If you can’t find what you’re looking for, you can instantly generate a quiz from any URL, Wikipedia article, PDFs, or even your hand-written notes.
Can Kahoot! be used for test preparation in college?
Kahoot! offers study tools for college students, study tools for high school students, and even study tools for young children on Kahoot! Kids.
Can Kahoot! help me with homework?
Kahoot! is a very effective homework helper, especially for learning new material or preparing class presentations. You can upload your homework and practice answering questions in fun and interactive formats. For presentations, you can sync your Google Slides or PowerPoints and enhance them with points and podiums to keep your class engaged.

Frequently Asked Questions
What are the best presentation tools for students?
The best presentation tools for students make it easier to plan, design, and deliver clear, engaging content without needing advanced technical skills. Kahoot!+ combines interactive slides, quizzes, polls, and AI-powered study tools in one platform, so you can structure your ideas, build your slides, and rehearse effectively for class presentations, group projects, and college presentations.
How can students make their presentations more engaging?
Engaging presentations are well structured, focused, and interactive. With Kahoot!, you can open with a quick poll or word cloud to introduce your topic, insert quiz questions between sections to check understanding, and use open-ended questions to prompt reflection and discussion. Keeping slides visually simple and using interactive questions to reinforce key points helps your audience follow your argument and remember the main takeaways.
Check out our full guide for creating engaging presentations here.
Is Kahoot! free for students?
Yes, Kahoot! is free for students! Students can create a free Kahoot! account to join games, create their own kahoots, and access online study tools for students such as flashcards and practice modes to prepare for exams and presentations. A Kahoot!+ subscription is optional and adds more advanced question types, AI-powered tools, and additional features for students who want a more comprehensive presentation and study toolkit.
Can students use AI tools to create presentations?
With Kahoot!+, you can use AI tools to turn notes or an assignment brief into a structured outline, suggest slide content, and generate quiz or poll questions aligned with your topic. These AI study tools for students support the planning and drafting process while leaving you in full control of the final wording, examples, and design of your presentation.

Turn notes into flashcards in seconds
Copy your notes into Kahoot! and use AI study tools to instantly turn notes into flashcards and interactive study sets—perfect for fast, on-the-go revision. With Kahoot!’s online flashcards, you can highlight key concepts, flip through cards on any device to study for exams anywhere, and mix flashcards with quiz questions for active recall. Generate practice tests with AI

Deepen your revision with proven exam study techniques
Pair your AI-powered revision with proven strategies for exam prep and better focus using Kahoot!+. Turn each topic into its own kahoot or study set, combine practice tests with flashcards in one place to strengthen recall, and use quick kahoots after classes or lectures to recap what you’ve just learned.

How to prepare for exams with Kahoot!+
Kahoot!+ gives you everything you need to plan, practice, and revise for exams in one place. Start by organizing each subject into its own kahoot or study set, using AI tools to turn your notes, slides, and past papers into questions and flashcards.

Can Kahoot! actually help me to get ready for my exams?

Frequently asked questions
Can I play Kahoot! for free?
Yes, you can host a kahoot for free with up to 10 players. If you want to play with more players, check our Kahoot!+ plans above.
Do players need a paid subscription?
No, players don’t need a subscription to play your kahoots, and can play without a Kahoot! account.
Where can I read more about the learning apps included in Kahoot!+ Bronze, Silver and Gold for students?
Visit these pages to find more information about Kahoot! DragonBox and Poio apps:
DragonBox math and chess apps
Poio learn to read app
How can I redeem my access to the learning apps included in Kahoot!+ Bronze, Silver and Gold for students?
To unlock your unlimited access to Kahoot! Learn to Read by Poio, Kahoot! Numbers by DragonBox, Kahoot! Big Numbers by DragonBox, Kahoot! Algebra by DragonBox, Kahoot! Algebra 2 by DragonBox, Kahoot! Geometry by DragonBox, Kahoot! Learn Chess by DragonBox, and Kahoot! Kids, download them from the App Store or Google Play and use your Kahoot! credentials to log in.
Alternatively, you can download all apps through the Kahoot! app. Open the app, log in to your account with Kahoot!+ Bronze, Silver or Gold subscription, and tap Download now from your home screen.
Download the Kahoot! app now for Android or iOS.
Can I play Kahoot! over video?
Yes, you can host a live kahoot via Skype, Google Meet, Microsoft Teams, Zoom, SharePlay or another video conferencing tool. Share your screen and the computer sound, while players join remotely with the game PIN from their devices.
Can I use Kahoot! in languages other than English?
Yes! You can write questions and answers in any language to create a native language experience for participants. You can also play, create and host kahoots, view reports, and more in Spanish, French, Brazilian Portuguese, German, Italian, Norwegian, Swedish, Danish, Dutch, Turkish, Polish, Japanese, Arabic, Ukrainian, Korean, Indonesian, Simplified Chinese, Traditional Chinese and Thai. Kahoot! is more accessible than ever!
Is there a monthly option?
Yes. Our student plans can be billed monthly. In order to view and purchase the monthly offer, switch the toggle right below the price cards to the Monthly option.
How can I share learning apps on multiple devices?
If you purchase a Kahoot! subscription that includes access to the learning apps, you can create up to 6 profiles on your account. You will need to use the same login details for each profile. You can read more about this here.
Do I get access to pre-made premium content (AccessPass) with my subscription?
Access to the pre-made premium content (AccessPass) is included with the Kahoot!+ Gold annual subscriptions. If you chose a different plan, your subscription does not include the AccessPass content.
On this pricing page you can see and buy feature subscriptions.To purchase the content subscription AcessPass go here. You can have both feature and content subscription at the same time. Read more about feature and content subscriptions
I can't find my current plan in the offer.
Do you have an active student subscription that isn’t visible on the pricing pages? Check the features of legacy plans for students.

Certification
Kahoot! Certified
for schools
Become Kahoot! Certified with our free program to help you transform your teaching. Learn how you can save time, simplify lesson planning, and create engaging learning experiences your students will love!
Get Certified Advanced Certificates
What is Kahoot! Certified for schools?
Our Kahoot! Certified for schools program will help you get confident with the basics through to becoming a Kahoot! Superhero. We have multiple exciting courses for you to complete!
You’ll get the chance to earn digital badges, score awesome diplomas, and claim professional learning credits.
Bronze
Beginner: 30 minutes
Effortlessly navigate the Kahoot! platform
Search and discover existing high-quality kahoots from our library
Launch and host a live kahoot in class or via video
Silver
Intermediate: 30 minutes
Create high quality-kahoots
Tailor your kahoots using a variety of question types
Assign kahoots and analyze Kahoot! reports
Gold
Advanced: 30 minutes
Apply a student-centric approach
Understand game options: solo game modes vs team modes
Understand how to use student passes
To become a Kahoot! superhero, complete these courses in sequence, unlocking new skills at each step!
Get certified now
Take your Kahoot! knowledge to the next level with these new, standalone courses
NEW! Kahoot! Energize
Intermediate: 30 minutes
Learn all about Kahoot! AI creation
Sync your Google slides and PowerPoint presentation
Transform your presentations with Kahoot! lecture mode
Earn the Energize certificate
NEW! Kahootopia!
Intermediate: 30 minutes
Discover how to bring Kahootopia! to life
Learn how to grow your island and unlock seasonal rewards
Boost engagement with Kahootopia! leagues
Earn the Kahootopia! certificate
NEW! AI generator
Intermediate: 30 minutes
Master the Kahoot!’s AI Generator
Learn how to quickly generate customized quizzes
Extract questions from your own PDF content
Earn the AI Generator certificate
Frequently asked questions
Who can take the courses?
Anyone with a Kahoot! teacher account can participate! The courses will most benefit those who are teaching, presenting, or training on Kahoot! in some way, but all Kahoot!’ers are welcome to take part.
Sign up for a Kahoot! account.
How do I take the courses?
The essential Kahoot! Certified for schools program currently consists of three tiered courses: Bronze (beginner), Silver (intermediate), and Gold (advanced), as well as standalone courses like the Energize course.
Each course consists of our new content format story, where you’ll read through tips and tricks and watch videos before taking part in a kahoot to recap the module for you to claim your diploma and badge.
How do I take the assignment and get certified?
Each course has a link to an assignment, with a Kahoot! challenge. To complete the assignment challenges, you’ll need to be signed in to your Kahoot! account so that we can identify you and email you your diploma and badge on completion of the course.
How do I get to the next course?
When you have passed an assignment, we’ll email you the diploma, badge, and the link to the next course. We’ll send this to the email registered to your Kahoot! account.
Can I claim PD credits?
Yes: we have designed the program so you could be eligible to claim professional development credits. On each course page we have specified how many hours are needed to finish each course tier. Moreover, our PDF study guides and the diploma (for passed courses) can be used as documentation to show completion of the Kahoot! Certified for schools program.

How you can use Kahoot! at your school or district
Collect valuable responses with feedback question types to gain insights, ideas, and opinions to enhance interaction and engagement during or after your lesson.
Recommended plan: Kahoot!+ Silver
Track learning progress with advanced reports to see which students answer questions correctly and who needs more help – download and combine reports to see class progress over time.
Recommended plan: Kahoot!+ Gold
Boost engagement and collaboration with new interactive game modes that require students to answer questions correctly and work together to accomplish their goals!
Recommended plan: Kahoot!+ Silver
Introduce new topics with slides and repeated questions that put all students on a level playing field and allow them to feel successful and motivated early on in the lesson.
Recommended plan: Kahoot!+ Silver
Reinforce learning with live and assigned review kahoots to test their knowledge of a topic in a fun, engaging, and low-pressure environment before formal assessment.
Recommended plan: Kahoot!+ Silver
