Recommended plan: Kahoot!+ Bronze
Frequently asked questions
Is the pricing listed per team or per teacher?
Unless specified otherwise in a special offer or promotion, price is listed per teacher.
Can someone else pay for my subscription?
You can request a Kahoot!+ Voucher. All you need to do is generate a link and share it with someone who is eager to help. This allows them to pay for Kahoot! subscription of your selection. You can generate the link in the Billing tab of your account settings.
Do you offer both monthly and annual billing?
The prices displayed on our website include both the annual cost and the monthly breakdown of the annual subscription. However, all these plans are billed upfront for the entire year. Currently, there is no option for monthly billing for any of our educational plans.
Is Word Cloud, Open-Ended Questions and Brainstorm included in my subscription?
These features are not currently available with our current individual K-12 school plans due to Children’s Data Privacy laws.
If you wish to use the aforementioned features you can upgrade to our EDU subscription.
What are the participant limits per game?
The participants limit differs based on what plan you’re on:
Go: Up to 10 participants per game
Teacher essentials: 40 participants per game
Bronze: Up to 50 participants per game
Silver: Up to 100 participants per game
Gold: Up to 200 participants per game
I’d like to collaborate with my colleagues. Is there a team plan available?
Absolutely! At a discounted price, admins can empower and connect educators across a school or district with versatile tools for blended and distance learning. Learn more about a Kahoot! EDU site license and request a personalized quote here.
I can't find my content, it was moved to another workspace. What is a workspace and how does it work?
With workspaces, you can organize your content and separate what’s shared with your team and what’s private. A workspace is where you can create, edit, and access kahoots, courses, reports, and groups. You can switch between workspaces anytime. Access to content and features differs based on which workspace you’re in. Learn more about Workspaces here.
What is AccessPass and how does it work?
Kahoot!+ AccessPass is the first premium content subscription, representing the launch of a global, digital marketplace on Kahoot!. The new subscription provides access to a comprehensive library of premium ready-to-use resources from Kahoot!, on a wide range of topics for all ages, to take learning, teaching, and having fun in any environment to the next level!
With Kahoot!+ AccessPass, you get:
Unlimited access to premium content for learning, entertainment, and engagement.
Full library with thousands of ready-made games and resources including exclusive collections.
Trusted content from verified creators and partners is added monthly.
Can I add students to a group?
Yes, you can add students to your groups! You can find more information about groups here.
Young students (U.S. students under 13 and non-U.S. students under 16) are unable to join or create groups.
What are student passes?
Student pass is a function that allows teachers to easily share access to our premium functionality geared towards students, including learning apps and enhanced creator functionality, by simply sharing a QR code or a link. More information here.
Where can I find Kahoot!’s W-9 form?
Download our signed W-8-BEN-E form here.
Does Kahoot! still offer a free version?
Yes, we offer Kahoot! Go, a free plan for teachers. It provides the essential tools for creating engaging lessons, motivating students, and tracking progress. Click here to explore the full list of features available with Kahoot! Go.
How do I get a verified Kahoot! Academy profile?
Apply to become a Verified educator and join the Kahoot! Academy community and marketplace for educators.
Does Kahoot! provide a sole source letter?
Please send us a request to schools@kahoot.com.
Can I use Kahoot! in languages other than English?
Yes! You can write questions and answers in any language to create a native language experience for participants. You can also play, create and host kahoots, view reports and more in Spanish, Brazilian Portuguese, French, German, Italian, Japanese, Dutch, Polish, Turkish, Norwegian, Swedish, Danish, Arabic, Ukrainian, Korean, Indonesian, Simplified Chinese, Traditional Chinese, Thai and Vietnamese. More languages are coming soon to our apps and website!
What is type answer question?
Kahoot! now supports a variety of open-ended question types to eliminate guesswork and test how well learners recall content without cues by asking them to type the correct short answer:
With type answer questions, you can test how well learners recall content without cues by asking them to type the correct short answer! Participants type in an answer which is validated instantly.
Can I get a site license?
Absolutely! At a discounted price, admins can empower and connect educators across a school or district with versatile tools for blended and distance learning. Learn more about a Kahoot! EDU site license and request a personalized quote here.
Are students able to share kahoots?
Yes, students above certain age can share their content without any constraints.
Young student accounts (U.S. students under 13 and non-U.S. students under 16) can present their own kahoots live to their class, but they cannot share (make public, share via link or with another account) their content.
If you want your children or students to share their question ideas with you, you can use the import questions from template feature.
My school is tax exempt. Why was I charged tax and how can I get rid of it?
We are currently updating our systems to reflect our customers’ tax-exemption status correctly. If you were charged tax while your organization is tax-exempt, please contact our Support team so that we can adjust this. We will need to confirm some details with you and will request the tax exemption certificate so it is recommended to have it prepared
How do I downgrade/upgrade my subscription?
To upgrade or downgrade, subscribe to a higher or lower tier plan on this pricing page or through prompts in the platform. Read more about upgrades and downgrades here.
You can upgrade anytime with an immediate effect.
I can't find my current plan in the offer.
Do you have an active school teacher subscription that isn’t visible on the pricing pages? Check the features of school (K-12) legacy plans here.

Kahoot! Cup
Book an intro of Kahoot! EDU
Kahoot! for schools
Assessment
Kahoot! goes beyond being a fun motivation or reward for students. From quick pulse checks to formative assessment and tracking class progress, it can help you capture actionable insights and target instruction in any learning environment.
Get Kahoot!+ Book an intro
Get started for free
How Kahoot! powers up assessment in class, virtually and in a blended learning environment
Pre-assess knowledge
Gather insights that will help you plan future lessons in the best possible way, aligned with where a class currently stands.
Review content
Engage students with content review in class and at home and identify topics that need reteaching.
Test practice and prep
Many teachers share that using Kahoot! for test practice and prep helps their students achieve better scores.
Reinforce challenging content
Identify knowledge gaps in your class and fine-tune instruction to reinforce these topics and help students master them.
Power up independent study
Assign student-paced kahoots that learners can complete to study and practice at home or in class.
Insights-driven instruction
Capture valuable analytics from kahoot reports to assess learning outcomes and class progress, and target your instruction.
“Analytics are huge! Kahoot! has been really helpful during the pandemic to check in on how students actually feel.”
Gustavo Lovato, AP Spanish and literature teacher
Read success story
“Kahoot! breaks down assessment analytics by class, question, and just about any metric you could imagine.”
Ryan Zamzow, chemistry teacher
Read success story
Key features for review and assessment
Run pulse-checks with quiz, polls and other questions
Collect valuable student feedback that you can act on immediately by using multiple-choice quiz, polls, word clouds and open-ended questions. For example, pre-assess knowledge, get an understanding of students’ expectations, and find out how they’re feeling in general.
Get actionable insights from reports
Visual reports on live games and student-paced challenges will help you assess how the class performed, identify difficult questions that need followup, and reinforce learning in a targeted way.
Assess class progress in real time
You can get a snapshot of how your class performed in a kahoot immediately after playing it, which gives you real-time visibility into learning progress.
Assign student-paced kahoots
In an assigned kahoot, learners see questions and answers on their screen, and they can play both in class and at home. You can turn off the question timer to promote accuracy, which makes this feature particularly helpful for content review.
Share insights with other educators
With a premium subscription, you can easily share reports with other teachers and admins from your school, giving them visibility into the learning progress.
Reuse existing kahoots for review
No need to create a new kahoot from scratch! You can duplicate and edit existing kahoots created by other teachers, pull questions from our question bank, or add a few finishing touches to your existing kahoots to optimize them for review.
Combine several reports
With a Kahoot! EDU subscription, you can combine several reports and see aggregated results. This will help you understand how well your class mastered a topic or series of topics throughout several lessons, or assess combined results across different classes.

Achieve end-to-end course engagement with Kahoot!
Discover the all-in-one toolkit you need for the new academic year, from lecture planning to exam season. Save time with AI-powered tools, elevate engagement with interactive questions and follow up on progress with reports.

Get started for free
Integrate Kahoot! into your course planning for engaging instruction
Save time with AI-powered tools
Build an entire lecture from start to finish by typing in a topic to get a variety of questions, slides, and images, you can add the language and the level of complexity as well. Transform lengthy PDFs into easy-to-digest kahoots to be used in class or sent to students as an assignment.
Get started
Turn presentations into interactive experiences with lecture mode
Our enhanced hosting experience makes presenting easier than ever! Seamlessly sync your slides and elevate them with Kahoot!’s interactivity, controlling questions, points, and podiums to perfectly match the pace and tone of your presentation. Include quick assessments and collect feedback to keep students engaged.
Get started
Engage students with multiple course formats
Energize students in face-to-face courses by hosting live kahoots, or create interactive learning experiences in asynchronous courses with assignments. For hybrid courses engage students joining remotely, allowing them to share their ideas and questions just the same as those attending in person.
Get started
Incorporate Kahoot! for assessment and review
Assess student learning with reports
Throughout the semester regularly check student learning outcomes. Assess prior learning at the start of the semester and pinpoint learning gaps by incorporating interactive question types throughout your lectures.
Get started
Collect student insights with feedback question types
Find out how familiar students are with a topic or theory by adding a poll question to start your kahoot. Capture student insights and ideas with open-ended questions or word clouds, and collect student feedback with the scale question type.
Get started
Foster independent study with student assignments
Let students increase the fun and engagement during independent study and review sessions with self-paced assignments that can be played in solo-mode. Just remove the countdown clock on questions. Reviewing assignment reports ahead of lectures allows instructors to identify learning gaps and adjust their instruction accordingly.
Get started
Prepare students ahead of exams with game modes
Boost the energy level during review sessions by testing students’s knowledge with multiple game modes and challenge students with friendly competition using team modes. Following the review sessions identify learning gaps with game mode reports.
Get started
Backed by peer-reviewed research
New research shows that using Kahoot! has a strong positive impact on students’ learning outcomes. Check out the report and other peer-reviewed research articles demonstrating the effects of Kahoot!
Integrative review and analysis of the effects of Kahoot! quiz games
A meta-analysis of independent research reveals that using Kahoot! on average shifts academic performance up by a full letter grade.
Download the report

Read a comparative demonstration of how Kahoot! is an effective tool to engage students in learning and increase knowledge retention.

Read the study

Cool math games: make learning math awesome with Kahoot! DragonBox
Give kids a unique, interactive experience to learn math on their own at home or in addition to their school curriculum with these cool math games. The full Kahoot! Kids experience, incl. Kahoot! DragonBox, is available with a Kahoot! Kids subscription.
Download Kahoot! Kids app >
Buy Kahoot! Kids
Discover the world of cool math games with DragonBox through these apps:
You get access to DragonBox math apps, and more educational games, with a Kahoot!+ subscription for personal use or Kahoot!+ Silver and EDU for teachers.
Kahoot! Multiplication by DragonBox
Turn the chore of memorizing the times tables into an awesome and captivating game experience.! Recommended for ages 8 and up.
Kahoot! Numbers
by DragonBox
Introduce your kids to math with fun activities that explain what numbers are and how they work. Recommended for ages 4-8.
Kahoot! Big Numbers by DragonBox
Help your kids understand long additions and subtractions through play and exploration. Recommended for ages 6-9.
Kahoot! Algebra by DragonBox
Give young learners a head start in math and algebra. This intuitive game teaches processes needed to solve basic linear equations. Recommended for ages 5 and up.
Kahoot! Algebra 2
by DragonBox
Help children gain confidence in algebra and math and improve their grades with this engaging game. Recommended for ages 9+.
Kahoot! Geometry by DragonBox (new!)
Learn geometry on an exciting learning adventure in the world of shapes. Recommended for ages 9 and up.
Kahoot! Learn Chess by DragonBox (new!)
Learn to play chess or improve your skills and become the grandmaster in your family! Recommended for ages 5 and up.
“...the first thing you should download on a tablet if you have kids 4-8 years old.”
Forbes
“The most impressive math education app I've seen…”
The New York Times
Why millions of parents, teachers, and children love Kahoot! DragonBox apps
Visual and intuitive learning
For young kids, numbers might look confusing at the beginning of their learning journey. That’s why DragonBox is designed to visualize math in a way kids can better understand it. Nooms, the main DragonBox characters are an example of this. These characters visualize what numbers are, how they behave and relate to each other in an easy-to-understand way. This makes the learning so intuitive that children can learn on their own while exploring the DragonBox universe.
From counting apples to algebra
The DragonBox apps cover a wide range of math topics, from math basics for young kids (Kahoot! DragonBox Numbers) to advanced algebra for school students (Kahoot! DragonBox Algebra 12+). Each app offers a unique interactive experience that engages children of all ages with learning through playing.
Research-based games made by experts
All DragonBox apps are backed with extensive pedagogy research. Our pedagogy studio is internationally recognized for its highly innovative math learning games. The team is composed of educational experts, dedicated teachers, several PhDs, and award-winning artists, who work together to create experiences that are both rigorous and fun.
A 100% safe gaming experience
The DragonBox universe is friendly and safe for learners. While playing, kids won’t be exposed to any ads or in-app purchases, and there are no distractions.
Get started with Kahoot! Kids today!

Book an intro of Kahoot! EDU
Inclusive learning with Kahoot! Stories from special education classrooms
At Kahoot!, we believe that education should be accessible to everyone, and inclusion is essential in every learning environment. That’s why we’re constantly working to enhance our platform’s accessibility and inclusivity, ensuring that all students, regardless of their abilities, can engage, learn, and thrive.
Unlock all of our premium features with Kahoot!+, our all-in-one teaching toolkit for engaging lessons, homework and assignments, review, and assessment.
Get Kahoot!+ Book an intro
Get started for free
Special education
Special education focuses on meeting the unique needs of students with disabilities, and while we provide the tools, the real magic occurs in the classroom. Special education teachers are the true heroes, creatively adapting Kahoot! features to support each student’s learning journey.
We’ve had the privilege of interviewing some of these dedicated educators to learn how they tailor Kahoot! to fit their students’ needs, making learning more engaging, inclusive, and impactful. Throughout this page, you’ll discover their inspiring stories as well as key Kahoot! features they use to create ideal learning sessions. Whether you’re a special education teacher or simply seeking ways to make learning more inclusive, we hope these insights inspire you to explore new possibilities with Kahoot! in your classroom.
Derek
Sign language & special education teacher, Florida, US
“Kahoot! is a vital part of my teaching toolbox, and I will continue to use it in every classroom I teach.”
Berit
Special Pedagog, Norway
“Kahoot! allows me to both teach and test my students. I can customize the slides and questions down to the smallest detail.”
Cheryl
Diverse Learner Teacher, US
“Kahoot! allows me to create inclusive learning by adapting content to meet each student’s specific needs.”
Sean
Common Core Teacher with IDD students, Norway
“For my IDD students, participation and shared activity are key. That’s why Kahoot!’s Accuracy mode is a perfect fit for us.”
Derek – sign language and special education teacher
Derek Smith is a sign language teacher in Orange County, Florida, primarily teaching American Sign Language as a second language at high school level. He also holds a degree in special education, and some of his students are hearing-impaired.
Kahoot! plays a major role in his lessons. Each week, he integrates interactive quizzes he has personally created, using them for formative and summative assessments, motivation, and even as a learning tool for homebound students. Derek’s kahoots reinforce sign recognition and comprehension. Most quizzes feature GIFs or videos of him signing words or sentences, prompting students to select the correct meaning.
He previously used still images but was thrilled when Kahoot! introduced video embedding. Since then, he has created hundreds of supporting videos for his YouTube channel and seamlessly integrates them directly into his kahoots, enhancing the learning experience for his students.
“Kahoot is a vital part of my teaching toolbox, and I will continue to use it in every classroom I teach.”
Check out Derek’s profile
Another game-changer for Derek was the use of GIFs instead of videos. This simple shift saved him significant time—no longer needing to navigate through YouTube for each question. Now, he also leverages GIFs in the answer options, allowing students to connect signs with words in both directions.
With years of experience, Derek has built a comprehensive library of Kahoot! quizzes covering all the units he teaches. The reporting tool within Kahoot! helps him track student progress, identify areas for improvement, and create study groups for those needing extra practice. He also encourages students to practice and use flashcards at home, reinforcing their learning beyond the classroom. Derek enjoys observing how engaged his students are with Kahoot! and appreciates the friendly competition it fosters. He states that Kahoot! is a vital part of his teaching toolbox, and he will continue to use it in every classroom he teaches.
Features that Derek finds helpful:
Images and GIFs
Adding images and GIFs to your kahoot questions make learning more accessible by adding visual support, simplifying complex concepts, and capturing students’ attention—especially those with learning differences or short attention spans. Learn how to use free images from our library or upload your own here!
Videos in kahoots
Adding videos to your kahoot can clarify key concepts, offer insights, or serve as an enjoyable pause between questions. You can effortlessly upload videos from YouTube or Vimeo to enrich your Kahoot! experience. Discover how to add videos here!
Reporting
Kahoot!’s reporting system offers valuable insights into student performance, assisting educators in tracking progress and identifying learning gaps. After each game, comprehensive reports display question accuracy, player scores, and overall class performance, making it easy to assess understanding.
Berit – special pedagog
A special education teacher in Norway, Berit Rostad, works with a group of 14 teenagers with various cognitive challenges. Every week she integrates Kahoot! into her teaching.
Some of her students have memory and reading challenges, and, by using pictures (both in the main questions and the answer options), she helps them process information in a way that suits them best. Berit takes pictures of recognizable things from the students’ immediate environment and includes them in her lessons.
She often uses slides to provide short snippets of information, followed by a question, which gives the students a sense of mastery and confidence through the instant repetition of key information. The variety in question types, especially Pin Answer, keeps the experience dynamic and engaging.
One of the highlights of Berit’s classroom is when they open a kahoot on Kahootopia! The surprise of opening the package creates joy and excitement in the group. Seeing their avatars represented on the island reinforces the sense of community and that each student’s contribution matters.
For Berit, Kahoot! more than just a quiz game – it provides repetitive learning in a fun and memorable way and, most importantly, ensures that every student feels included and valued.
“Kahoot! allows me to both teach and test my students. I can customize the slides and questions down to the smallest detail.”
Check out Berit's kahoot
Features that Berit finds helpful:
Multiple question types
Engaging every student is key to meaningful learning. If you are new to Kahoot!, begin with the basics and learn how easily you can create your first kahoot here. If you are familiar with the basics, check out how you can use different question types here.
Images as answers
Visual support helps engage learners, reinforce understanding, and create a more adaptable learning experience. Learn how to use free images from our image library or upload your own to tailor your kahoots to your students’ needs. Get started here.
Kahootopia!
Celebrate class achievement and inclusive learning with collective rewards in Kahootopia! The ultimate tool for collaborative learning is now available for free to all teachers. Learn how to create your first island here.
Virpi – special education teacher
Every day, Virpi Roine from Finland, a dedicated special education teacher, uses Kahoot! to engage her class of ten 15-year-old neurodiverse students.
– Image is for illustration purposes only
For them, Kahoot! isn’t just a learning tool—it’s a source of excitement, inclusion, and confidence. The interactive format keeps them motivated, while the friendly competition and instant feedback make learning fun and accessible. The combination of visuals, sounds, and interactive elements helps students with concentration challenges stay engaged. Gamification allows students to participate at their own pace, fostering confidence, while instant responses reinforce learning in real time, providing positive encouragement.
They are exploring different game modes, with the podium often serving as a great motivator. However, the teacher also values the flexibility to hide the podium when needed, with the option of using Lecture Mode to create a more relaxed learning environment. She also appreciates the nickname generator, which ensures a safe and positive environment through placing all students on a level playing field. With these features, Kahoot! helps Virpi create a fun, inclusive, and supportive space where every student can thrive in their own unique way!
“Kahoot! helps me create an inclusive space where every one of my students can thrive.”
Features that Virpi finds helpful:
Kahoot game modes
The gamified format in our game modes encourages all students to actively participate. Learn more about the different game modes, and find one that would suit your students.
Nickname generator
The Nickname Generator fosters a safe and supportive learning environment by generating fun, random nicknames for students. This feature promotes privacy and inclusivity, ensuring that everyone feels comfortable participating. Learn more here.
Lecture mode
Lecture mode enables teachers to manage their presentations without needing to focus on the scoreboard and podium displays. Removing these competitive elements allows students to take time to think and answer, prioritizing accuracy over speed. Discover everything about it here.
Sean – Common Core IDD teacher
Sean Murphy, a Norwegian Common Core teacher who works with students with intellectual and developmental disabilities (IDD), has seen firsthand how Kahoot! can transform his classroom.
Many of his students are on the autism spectrum and benefit from clear structure, pre-planning, and repetition. Sean uses Kahoot! frequently in larger groups and one-on-one sessions as a simple, fun wrap-up activity. It’s a perfect way to bring everyone together, and he finds that what they do in the kahoot is often what they remember best.
With each student following their own curriculum, Kahoot!’s flexibility is a game-changer. Sean finds it’s easy to create multiple versions of a single kahoot to fit his students’ needs. The sharing culture among his colleagues means he can easily share a kahoot with them, and they can adapt it for their own students. That collaboration goes both ways.
He emphasizes how simple it is to create customized versions of a kahoot, removing the timer and points to keep things low-stress. Sean also uses a lot of images and reads questions aloud to help with comprehension. He loves the nickname generator because it helps students feel more comfortable. It removes the need for them to write their own name or create a nickname, and helps them remain anonymous.
When Kahoot! launched Accuracy mode, it was a perfect fit for Sean’s students. Unlike the classic mode, this version rewards correct answers over speed. He finds this makes the activity feel more relaxed and inclusive. For his students, participation is the most important thing, and seeing their avatar get a medal—even a bronze one—is a huge reward.
“For my IDD students, participation and shared activity are key. That’s why Kahoot!’s Accuracy mode is a perfect fit for us. It feels more relaxed, inclusive, and learning-focused, and when they see their avatar get a medal, that’s always a reward.”
